Model before rewriting:

find x: int(-5..2)
find y: int(-5..2)
find z: int(-5..2)

such that

sum([|(sum([(x / 2),y;int(1..)]) - z)|,(|y| / 2);int(1..)]) = 10

z
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
z
new variables:
  find z#sign_magnitude_1: bool
  find z#sign_magnitude_2: bool
  find z#sign_magnitude_3: bool
  find z#sign_magnitude_4: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(z#sign_magnitude_3),and([!(z#sign_magnitude_2),or([!(z#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
  (z#sign_magnitude_4) -> (or([!(z#sign_magnitude_3),and([!(z#sign_magnitude_2),or([!(z#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]))
  (!(z#sign_magnitude_4)) -> (and([!(z#sign_magnitude_3),or([!(z#sign_magnitude_2),and([!(z#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]))
  (z#sign_magnitude_4) -> (or([z#sign_magnitude_1,z#sign_magnitude_2,z#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)]))
  or([and([SATInt(SignMagnitude, [z#sign_magnitude_1,z#sign_magnitude_2,z#sign_magnitude_3,z#sign_magnitude_4;int(1..)] [-5, 2]) >= -5,SATInt(SignMagnitude, [z#sign_magnitude_1,z#sign_magnitude_2,z#sign_magnitude_3,z#sign_magnitude_4;int(1..)] [-5, 2]) <= 2;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
